- **Summary**: 先前Transformer学习动力学分析局限于特定任务。提出统一归纳推理任务类的理论框架，证明训练动力学限制在低维不变流形上，可用少量坐标刻画。表征数据统计如何控制上下文学习与权重学习的竞争，以及随机初始化决定胜出电路。

<h3 id="335">[335] Metacognition in LLMs: Foundations, Progress, and Opportunities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.11881](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11881) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Areeb Gani, Jacqueline Lu, Jordan Thomas, Mark Steyvers, Arman Cohan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Metacognition, Survey
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition)
- **Summary**: 大语言模型何时、如何及多大程度展现元认知能力尚不明确。提供首个关于LLM元认知的全面综述，分类总结测量方法、增强技术和研究发现。系统梳理了应用、开放问题和未来方向。

- **arXiv**: [2503.14499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14499) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Kwa, Ben West, Joel Becker, Amy Deng, Katharyn Garcia, Max Hasin, Sami Jawhar, Megan Kinniment, Nate Rush, Sydney Von Arx, Ryan Bloom, Thomas Broadley, Haoxing Du, Brian Goodrich, Nikola Jurkovic, Luke Harold Miles, Seraphina Nix, Tao Lin, Chris Painter, Neev Parikh, David Rein, Lucas Jun Koba Sato, Hjalmar Wijk, Daniel M. Ziegler, Elizabeth Barnes, Lawrence Chan

- **Summary**: AI基准测试性能的现实意义不明确。提出“50%任务完成时间视界”指标，测量人类完成软件任务的时间，并与AI模型比较。前沿AI的50%时间视界约50分钟，每7个月翻倍，预测5年内可自动化目前人类需一个月完成的软件任务。

<h3 id="337">[337] Trojan Horse Prompting: Jailbreaking Conversational Multimodal Models by Forging Assistant Message</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.04673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04673) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wei Duan, Li Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, Safety Alignment
- **Summary**: 现有对话大模型安全机制存在不对称性：模型被训练拒绝有害用户请求，但对自己声称的历史消息缺乏怀疑。本文提出"特洛伊木马提示"攻击方法，通过在API提供的对话历史中伪造模型自身的历史消息来注入恶意负载。在Gemini-2.0-flash-preview-image-generation上，该方法取得了比现有用户轮次越狱方法更高的攻击成功率，揭示了对会话上下文完整性进行协议级验证的必要性。

<h3 id="338">[338] LLM-Driven Collaborative Model for Untangling Commits via Explicit and Implicit Dependency Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.16395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bo Hou, Xin Tan, Kai Zheng, Fang Liu, Yinghao Zhu, Li Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Review
- **Summary**: 现有提交碎解方法难以区分显式与隐式依赖。本文提出ColaUntangle，一个基于LLM多智能体协作框架，分别由显式依赖智能体、隐式依赖智能体和评审智能体通过迭代协作完成碎解。在C#和Java数据集上，相比最优基线分别提升44%和82%。

<h3 id="339">[339] InqEduAgent: Adaptive AI Learning Partners with Gaussian Process Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.03174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03174) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wen-Xi Yang, Tian-Fang Zhao, Guan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Personalized Learning, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ACMLC 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：基于经验或规则的伙伴分配导致知识扩展有限且适应性低。方法：提出InqEduAgent，一种LLM驱动的生成式代理框架，结合高斯过程增强匹配机制，根据学习者的认知和评估特征进行自适应选择。结果：在不同学习场景和LLM配置下均取得优越性能。

<h3 id="340">[340] Mini Amusement Parks (MAPs): A Testbed for Modelling Business Decisions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.15830](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15830) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Stéphane Aroca-Ouellette, Ian Berlot-Attwell, Panagiotis Lymperopoulos, Abhiramon Rajasekharan, Tongqi Zhu, Herin Kang, Kaheer Suleman, Sam Pasupalak
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Evaluation, World Model, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Skyfall-Research/MAPs)
- **Summary**: 现有基准大多孤立评估决策能力，缺乏综合性的决策能力评估平台。本文提出Mini Amusement Parks (MAPs)，一个游乐园模拟器，用于评估智能体在长程规划、空间推理和世界建模等方面的能力。实验发现人类在简单和中等模式下分别超过最强LLM智能体6.5倍和9.8倍。

<h3 id="341">[341] Do Implicit Personalization and Explicit Styles Conflict? PsPLUG: A Lightweight Plug-in for Balancing Personalization and Style in Customized LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06362) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yutong Song, Jiang Wu, Shaofan Yuan, Chengze Shen, Jian Wang, Yu Wang, Nikil Dutt, Amir M. Rahmani
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Controllable Generation
- **Summary**: 个性化LLM在遵循显式风格指令时可能削弱用户特征（个性化崩溃）。本文提出PsPLUG轻量级插件，通过学习风格控制后的用户残差，并在推理时调节个性化强度。实验表明PsPLUG能更好地保留用户偏好，同时精确控制个性化与风格的平衡。

<h3 id="342">[342] BizFinBench.v2: Towards Reliable LLMs in Finance via Real-User Data and Offline/Online Bilingual Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06401) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xin Guo, Rongjunchen Zhang, Guilong Lu, Xuntao Guo, Shuai Jia, Zhi Yang, Liwen Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation

- **Summary**: 现有金融领域基准多基于模拟数据，与真实场景存在较大差距。本文提出BizFinBench.v2，首个基于中美股市真实用户查询的线上线下双语基准，包含28,860道问题。测试表明GPT-5仅达61.5%准确率，远未达到实际业务要求的84.8%，DeepSeek-R1投资效能最佳。

<h3 id="343">[343] FIRE-Bench: Evaluating AI Agents on the Rediscovery of Scientific Insights</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02905) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhen Wang, Fan Bai, Zhongyan Luo, Jinyan Su, Kaiser Sun, Xinle Yu, Jieyuan Liu, Kun Zhou, Claire Cardie, Mark Dredze, Zhiting Hu, Eric P. Xing
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Agent Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 评估科学发现智能体时，现有基准要么依赖LLM评判，要么使用孤立性能指标，缺乏严谨性和灵活性兼顾的评估框架。本文提出FIRE-Bench，让智能体从高层次研究问题出发，自主完成实验设计、代码实现和结论推导，以重现已发表论文的发现。评估显示最强智能体F1不足50%，且方差大、失败模式重复。

<h3 id="344">[344] JADE: Expert-Grounded Dynamic Evaluation for Open-Ended Professional Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06486) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lanbo Lin, Jiayao Liu, Tianyuan Yang, Li Cai, Yuanwu Xu, Lei Wei, Sicong Xie, Guannan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM-as-a-Judge, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/smiling-world/JADE)
- **Summary**: 开放型专业任务的评估面临固定评分标准与LLM评判不稳定之间的矛盾。本文提出JADE双层评估框架：第一层编码专家知识作为评估技能，第二层进行声明级评估并通过证据依赖门控过滤无效结论。实验表明JADE提升了评估稳定性，并揭示了被整体LLM评估器忽略的关键失败模式。

<h3 id="345">[345] A Model-Free Universal AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23242) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yegon Kim, Juho Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Model-Free RL
- **Summary**: 通用强化学习中所有已知最优智能体都是基于模型的。本文提出Universal AI with Q-Induction (AIQI)，首个被证明在通用RL中渐近ε-最优的无模型智能体。AIQI对分布性动作价值函数进行通用归纳，并在grain of truth条件下证明其渐近最优性。

<h3 id="346">[346] Where Experts Disagree, Models Fail: Detecting Implicit Legal Citations in French Court Decisions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22973) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Avrile Floro, Tamara Dhorasoo, Soline Pellez, Nils Holzenberger
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 计算法学在大规模应用中需区分真正法律推理与表面相似性。本文聚焦于检测法国民法典隐性引用（法院适用但未明文提及），发布1,015条段落-条款对基准。核心发现是专家分歧本身具有信息性：模型在分歧案例上集中犯错，最佳集成F1为0.70，而非监督排名可达到76%精确率。

<h3 id="347">[347] When Sensing Varies with Contexts: Context Probing for Tactile Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning</h3>

<h3 id="348">[348] Open, Reliable, and Collective: A Community-Driven Framework for Tool-Using AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.00137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00137) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hy Dang, Quang Dao, Meng Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, Tool-Augmented Agent, LLM Agent

<h3 id="349">[349] GrandCode: Achieving Grandmaster Level in Competitive Programming via Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02721) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="350">[350] Agentic Forecasting using Sequential Bayesian Updating of Linguistic Beliefs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18576) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kevin Murphy
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Event Prediction, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 现有LLM预测系统通常将所有检索证据添加到无结构的上下文中。本文提出贝叶斯语言预测器(BLF)，使用半结构化的语言信念状态结合数值概率和自然语言摘要，并通过层次化多试验聚合和校准。在ForecastBench上超越所有公开方法，消融实验验证了各组件贡献。

<h3 id="351">[351] Algorithm Selection with Zero Domain Knowledge via Text Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19753) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Stefan Szeider
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Algorithm Selection, Text Embedding
- **Summary**: 算法选择通常依赖手工特征。本文提出ZeroFolio，仅通过预训练文本嵌入对原始实例文件进行序列化、嵌入和加权k近邻选择，无需领域知识。在11个ASlib场景中，ZeroFolio在9个上优于基于手工特征的随机森林，在ASlib竞赛结果上接近AutoFolio。

<h3 id="352">[352] Ideological Bias in LLMs' Economic Causal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.21334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21334) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Donggyu Lee, Hyeok Yun, Jungwon Kim, Junsik Min, Sungwon Park, Sangyoon Park, Jihee Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CL; cs.LG; econ.GN
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Detection, Causal Reasoning
- **Venue**: COLM 2026
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在经济因果推理中可能表现出系统性意识形态偏见。本文通过扩展EconCausal基准，在10,490个因果三元组中识别出1,056个意识形态争议案例，评估20个LLM。发现争议案例更难，且模型在结果倾向干预主义方向时准确率更高，错误也偏向干预主义方向，提示需要方向感知评估。

<h3 id="353">[353] Recursive Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.25917](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25917) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaru Zou, Rui Pan, Ruizhong Qiu, Pan Lu, Shizhe Diao, Jindong Jiang, Hanghang Tong, Tong Zhang, Markus J. Buehler, Jingrui He, James Zou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体系统缺乏递归扩展的问题，提出RecursiveMAS框架，通过RecursiveLink模块实现智能体间的潜在空间递归协作，并设计内外环学习算法进行联合优化。实验表明，该方法在数学、科学、医学等9个基准测试中平均准确率提升8.3%，推理速度提升1.2-2.4倍，token使用减少34.6%-75.6%。

<h3 id="354">[354] A Low-Latency Fraud Detection Layer for Detecting Adversarial Interaction Patterns in LLM-Powered Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01143) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sheldon Yu, Yingcheng Sun, Hanqing Guo, Qianqian Tong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体容易受到对抗性交互攻击的问题，提出一种低延迟欺诈检测层，利用运行时特征和XGBoost分类器对交互轨迹进行风险建模。实验表明，该方法比基于LLM的检测器快9倍以上，适用于实时部署。

<h3 id="355">[355] 2.5-D Decomposition for LLM-Based Spatial Construction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07066) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paul Whitten, Li-Jen Chen, Sharath Baddam
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuro-Symbolic AI, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在三维空间构建任务中坐标错误频发的问题，提出一种神经符号流水线：LLM仅规划二维平面，垂直位置由确定性执行器计算。在Build What I Mean基准上达到94.6%的结构准确率，并成功迁移到边缘硬件。

<h3 id="356">[356] EmbodiSkill: Skill-Aware Reflection for Self-Evolving Embodied Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10332) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruofei Ju, Xinrui Wang, Xin Ding, Yifan Yang, Hao Wu, Shiqi Jiang, Qianxi Zhang, Hao Wen, Xiangyu Li, Weijun Wang, Kun Li, Yunxin Liu, Haipeng Dai, Wei Wang, Ting Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Skill Learning, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 问题：现有技能自我进化方法在具身环境中无法区分技能内容错误和执行失误。方法：提出EmbodiSkill，一种免训练框架，通过技能感知反思和有针对性修订实现技能自我进化。结果：在ALFWorld上使用冻结的Qwen3.5-27B执行器达到93.28%任务成功率，比直接使用GPT-5.2作为代理提高31.58%。

<h3 id="357">[357] Learning Developmental Scaffoldings to Guide Self-Organisation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14998) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Milton L. Montero, Elias Najarro, Jakob Schauser, Sebastian Risi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; eess.SY; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Neural Cellular Automata, Self-Organization
- **Venue**: ALife 2026
- **Summary**: 研究生物发育中信息卸载到初始条件的现象，提出联合学习自组织规则和预模式的神经细胞自动机（NCA）模型。信息论分析表明，联合学习可提升鲁棒性、编码容量和对称性破缺能力。

<h3 id="358">[358] MindClaw: Closed-Loop Embodied Mental-State Reasoning for Precision Intervention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruoxuan Zhang, Qiaoqiao Wan, Zhengguang Wang, Chenghao Yu, Hongxia Xie, Jianlong Fu, Wen-Huang Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Theory of Mind
- **Summary**: 针对具身智能体缺乏实时闭环心智推理能力的问题，提出MindClaw框架，结合信念记忆、认知触发技能和动作生成，实现在适当时候提供帮助。实验表明，直接VLM基线在任务意识和干预校准上不足，MindClaw表现最佳。

<h3 id="359">[359] Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Alansary, Molham Mohamed, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Curriculum Learning, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在医疗问答中难以适应病例严重程度的问题，提出严重度感知的多模型框架，采用三阶段课程学习和基于BERTScore的响应选择。微调后BERTScore达90.30%，优于基线模型。

<h3 id="360">[360] When Does Delegation Beat Majority? A Delegation-Based Aggregator for Multi-Sample LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08098) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yasushi Sakai, Allen Song, Kent Larson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多数投票丢弃样本内熵和推理几何信息的问题，提出传播代理投票（PPV）委托聚合方法。在MMLU-Pro上比多数投票高1.5个百分点，能够推翻错误多数。

<h3 id="361">[361] TouchThinker: Scaling Tactile Commonsense Reasoning to the Open World with Large-scale Data and Action-aware Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11637](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11637) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kailin Lyu, Di Wu, Pengwei Zhang, Yuhang Zheng, Yingxin Lai, Long Xiao, Kangyi Wu, Pengna Li, Chen Gao, Lianyu Hu, Xiaobin Hu, Jie Hao, Ce Hao, Weihao Yuan, Shuicheng Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Tactile Sensing, Commonsense Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lvkailin0118/TouchThinker)
- **Summary**: 针对触觉常识推理在开放世界扩展中的数据不足和表示低效问题，构建百万级多源触觉数据集TouchThinker-1M，并提出动作感知建模机制。在多个数据集上取得竞争性性能。

<h3 id="362">[362] NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.27826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shiyun Zhao, Xinwei Song, Tianyu Guo, Xiaomeng Gao, Mingyuan Liu, Xu Han, Yuanyuan Zhang, Zhenliang Zhang, Xue Feng, Bo Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Social Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型在具身规划中忽视隐含社会规范的问题，构建NormAct基准，提出NormPerceptor上下文线索生成器。模型在显式目标达成率67.3%，而规范遵守率仅26.4%，线索生成器将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。

<h3 id="363">[363] Characterizing Large Language Model Agentic Workflows: A Study on N8n Ecosystem</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29116) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yutian Tang, Yuming Zhou, Huaming Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Engineering, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体工作流在低代码平台缺乏实证研究的问题，分析6000多个n8n工作流，揭示其结构、工具使用和可靠性机制。发现显式可靠性机制（如回退路径、人工审批）相对少见。

<h3 id="364">[364] Safety from Honesty in a Disinterested AI Predictor</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29657) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yoshua Bengio, Oliver Richardson, Tomáš Gavenčiak, Michael Cohen, Rory Svarc, Damiano Fornasiere, Gael Gendron, David Hyland, Aton Kamanda, Adam Oberman, Francis Rhys Ward, Anna Gavenčiak, Jacob Livingston Slosser, Vincent Mai, Iulian Serban, Joumana Ghosn
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Alignment, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 针对训练过程可能导致隐式代理的问题，提出科学家AI预测器（SAI Predictor）的形式化安全论证。通过认识语境化和后验目标，证明其产生有害预测的概率很小，安全性和准确性共同支持。

<h3 id="365">[365] FARS: A Fully Automated Research System Deployed at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.31651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31651) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiong Tang, Tianxiang Sun, Xiangkun Hu, Xiangyang Liu, Yiran Chen, Yunfan Shao, Bobo Li, Changze Lv, Cheng Xu, Chengsong Huang, Chunyang Li, Dizhan Xue, Hao Bai, Haodong Duan, Hengquan Guo, Hongyang He, Hongyi Chen, Hui Shen, Jiahao Yuan, Jiankai Sun, Jikang Cheng, Jinfeng Xu, Jingqi Tong, Jingye Chen, Jinxiu Liu, Jixuan Leng, Junchi Yu, Kaixun Jiang, Kun Xiang, Kunpeng Yao, Lang Feng, Liangqi Yuan, Longsen Gao, Meng Li, Qi Jia, Qiushi Sun, Shengyuan Ding, Shizhan Gong, Siru Zhong, Terry Jingchen Zhang, Tianle Gu, Tianyi Liang, Weijie Liu, Weikai Yang, Weizhi Fei, Xin Wang, Xinpeng Liu, Xuanwen Ding, Yihong Tang, Yuanli Wang, Yukun Jiang, Yuming Yang, Zhengbao He, Zhikai Chen, Zhikun Xu, Zhuang Li, Zihao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Automated Research
- **Summary**: 针对自动科研系统局限于选定示例的问题，提出FARS全自动科研系统，通过阶段专用智能体和共享工作空间生成166篇完整论文。282份评审显示其能产出可评阅甚至优秀的AI/ML研究工件，但也暴露出实验范围窄、完整性等问题。

<h3 id="366">[366] Agentic generation of verifiable rules for deterministic, self-expanding reaction classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01061) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daniel Armstrong, Maarten Dobbelaere, Valentas Olikauskas, Helena Avila, Octavian Susanu, Jérôme Waser, Philippe Schwaller
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Molecular Generation, Reaction Classification
- **Summary**: 针对化学反应规则手工编码且固定不变的问题，提出多智能体LLM流水线，在验证循环中自动编写规则。将标准分类体系从68类扩展到14073类，对未见过反应分类准确率97.7%，可动态扩展。

<h3 id="367">[367] Separating Expert Retention from Autonomous Source Inference in Raw-ECG-Replay-Free Continual ECG Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01674) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yufan Lu, Xinhui Liu, Chenyang Xu, Yuxi Zhou, Hao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Medical AI, Time Series Classification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yufanlu221/IRFE-ECG)
- **Summary**: 针对多源心电图部署中无法保留原始数据的问题，提出IRFE-ECG方法：冻结特征、增加专家分类器，并用轻量路由器进行源推断。源感知专家选择达0.7915 Macro-F1，但自主源推断是主要瓶颈。

<h3 id="368">[368] SUNTA: Hierarchical Video Prediction with Surprise-based Chunking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02087](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02087) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tomoshi Iiyama, Masahiro Suzuki, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Prediction, State Space Model, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对层次状态空间模型分块边界检测不准的问题，提出基于惊喜的嵌套时间抽象（SUNTA），采用解耦训练和内部不一致作为惊喜度量。在2D和3D视频预测任务中，SUNTA在250步内保持准确预测，而基线前10步即退化。

<h3 id="369">[369] Agent Step Value: Auditing Evaluator-Channel Reversals in Black-Box Agent Traces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04419) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrew Zhang, Chengzhan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Evaluation, Step Reward Audit
- **Summary**: 论文指出评估器导出的步奖励在不同评分渠道间符号可能反转，现有方法无法隔离状态与渠道的交互。提出Agent Step Value (ASV)作为渠道索引的目标边际增益，通过回放完整面识别缺失的状态×渠道交互。在PubMed开放问答中，直接评分均值+0.163，外部视图-0.160，交互-0.323；507/1004过渡交叉零；ASV提供了可运输审计。

<h3 id="370">[370] MoP-JEPA: Hard-Assigned Predictor Mixtures for Stochastic JEPA World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05238) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhi Song, Ximing Xing, Zhenchao Tang, hanbo Huang, Weilong Yan, Tianxu Lv, Minghao Yang, Zhongzheng Niu, Bing He, Lusheng Wang, Jianhua Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, JEPA
- **Summary**: JEPA世界模型用单一回归器预测下一潜在状态，在随机转换下可能没有有效后继。提出MoP-JEPA，使用K个硬分配头和一个上下文路由器产生有限候选集。在OGBench上单输出预测成功率仅0.02-0.09，MoP-JEPA达到0.85；提出verified-route success指标，MoP-JEPA在三个迷宫中领先。

<h3 id="371">[371] When do prophets profit in prediction markets?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.06166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06166) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anri Gu, Nicole Kagan, Alec Sun, Jibang Wu, Haifeng Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Prediction Markets, Economic Modeling
- **Summary**: 经典预测市场理论仅针对AMM设计，但现实交易所使用限价订单簿，导致精确性与利润关系不明。建立预测准确性与盈利之间的正式等价性，提出“proper”投注策略。在AI模型数千次预测中，该策略唯一可靠；在Kalshi上一个月部署获得+80.33% ROI，夏普比率3.35。

<h3 id="372">[372] Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.07405](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vikas Reddy, Sumanth Reddy Challaram, Abhishek Basu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 工具使用的LLM代理可能违反策略导致静默错误状态。提出轻量级干预：确定性只读预执行门，检查调用和当前状态后允许写入。四门套件在gpt-4o-mini上从29.6%提升至42.0%，在gpt-5.2上从61.2%提升至71.6%。

<h3 id="373">[373] Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.08964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zongxia Li, Zhongzhi Li, Yucheng Shi, Ruhan Wang, Junyao Yang, Zhichao Liu, Xiyang Wu, Anhao Li, Yue Yu, Ninghao Liu, Lichao Sun, Haotao Mi, Leowei Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Long-Horizon Planning, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有终端基准聚焦短任务仅评估最终结果，忽略中间进度。提出Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench，包含46个长时域任务，分解为细粒度子任务提供密集中间奖励。最强模型在0.95阈值下pass@1为15.2%，完美阈值10.9%，平均通过率4.3%和1.7%。

<h3 id="374">[374] LongMedBench: Benchmarking Medical Agents for Long-Horizon Clinical Decision-Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09322) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihan Xu, Yanzhen Chen, Xiaocheng Zhang, Zhiting Fan, Weiqi Zhai, Hongxia Xu, Zuozhu Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Benchmark, Clinical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有医学代理评估强调短上下文知识QA，忽略医疗的纵向性。提出LongMedBench，基于MIMIC-IV的长期临床决策基准，含335名患者。实验显示LLM能利用显式时间戳但隐含时间推理困难；RAG和记忆系统改善信息检索但决策依赖即时上下文。

<h3 id="375">[375] Research on Cross-media Science and Technology Information Data Retrieval</h3>

- **Authors**: Yang Jiang, Zhe Xue, Ang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Multimodal Retrieval
- **Summary**: 传统科技信息检索系统仅支持单模态和关键词匹配。研究基于深度语义特征的跨媒体科技信息检索系统，讨论了系统设计和趋势。

<h3 id="376">[376] Research on Intellectual Property Resource Profile and Evolution Law</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2204.06221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.06221) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuhui Wang, Yingxia Shao, Ang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="377">[377] Profiling and Evolution of Intellectual Property</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2204.09333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.09333) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bowen Yu, Yingxia Shao, Ang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Knowledge Extraction
- **Summary**: 科技政策资源提取困难。针对科技政策领域，介绍相关技术和发展，帮助降低信息获取成本。

<h3 id="378">[378] HiQA: A Hierarchical Contextual Augmentation RAG for Multi-Documents QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.01767](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01767) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyue Chen, Pengyu Gao, Jiangjiang Song, Xiaoyang Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering, Multi-Document Reasoning
- **Summary**: 面对大量难以区分文档时RAG检索精度有限。提出HiQA，一个多文档QA框架，结合级联元数据和多路由检索机制，在多文档环境中达到SOTA。

<h3 id="379">[379] Constrained Reinforcement Learning for Safe Heat Pump Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2409.19716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19716) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Baohe Zhang, Lilli Frison, Thomas Brox, Joschka Bödecker
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Constrained Optimization, Energy Management
- **Venue**: European Control Conference 2026
- **Summary**: 建筑供暖系统优化可形式化为约束优化问题，但RL数据需求大。提出建筑模拟器I4B，应用无模型约束RL算法CSAC-LB，展示了数据探索效率、约束满足和性能。

<h3 id="380">[380] Training on Irrelevant States Implies Data Augmentation: Generalization in Contextual MDPs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.03565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03565) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Max Weltevrede, Caroline Horsch, Matthijs T.J. Spaan, Wendelin Böhmer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Domain Generalization
- **Summary**: 在上下文MDP零样本策略迁移中，训练额外状态可提高泛化但可能降低值函数精度。提出Explore-Go方法，在训练开始时引入纯探索阶段，可结合多种算法，在多个泛化基准上提升测试性能。

<h3 id="381">[381] On Occlusions in Video Action Detection: Benchmark Datasets And Training Recipes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.19553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19553) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rajat Modi, Vibhav Vineet, Yogesh Singh Rawat
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Action Recognition, Occlusion Handling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rajatmodi62/OccludedActionBenchmark)
- **Summary**: 研究视频动作检测中遮挡的影响，引入五个新基准数据集。发现Transformer可超越CNN，胶囊组件可绑定未见过的遮挡物。模型在遮挡下性能提升32.3%等。

<h3 id="382">[382] Asynchronous Perception Machine For Efficient Test-Time-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.20535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20535) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rajat Modi, Yogesh Singh Rawat
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 提出异步感知机(APM)，一种高效的测试时训练架构，可非对称顺序处理图像块并编码语义。在OOD识别上具有竞争力，可扩展到2D图像产生语义聚类。

<h3 id="383">[383] Training-Free, Identity-Preserving Image Editing for Fashion Pose Alignment and Normalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.13692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13692) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Potito Aghilar, Vito Walter Anelli, Michelantonio Trizio, Eugenio Di Sciascio, Tommaso Di Noia
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Diffusion Model, Fashion AI
- **Summary**: 非刚性姿态调整（如服装）的编辑困难，需保持身份且无训练数据。提出FashionRepose，无需训练的保持身份编辑管线，用于时尚行业。已部署到全球时尚公司OVS，处理超3万件长袖服装。

<h3 id="384">[384] Disentangling Feature Structure: A Mathematically Provable Two-Stage Training Dynamics in Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.20681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20681) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zixuan Gong, Shijia Li, Yong Liu, Jiaye Teng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: Transformer训练中可能出现两阶段动态（语法→语义），现有理论缺乏特征级分析。理论证明了两阶段训练动态如何发生，基于解耦的两类型特征（如语法和语义），是第一个关于特征级两阶段优化过程的严格结果。

<h3 id="385">[385] Hyperflux: Pruning Reveals Importance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.05349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eugen Barbulescu, Antonio Alexoaie, Lucian Busoniu
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Network Pruning
- **Summary**: 现有网络剪枝方法多注重经验结果而缺乏对剪枝过程的理解。提出Hyperflux方法，将剪枝建模为基于通量和压力的连续演化系统，并引入压力调度器实现目标稀疏度。在多个数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果。

<h3 id="386">[386] Efficient Q-Learning and Actor-Critic Methods for Robust Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.07040](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07040) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Xu, Swetha Ganesh, Vaneet Aggarwal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Robust MDP, Q-Learning, Actor-Critic
- **Summary**: 针对分布鲁棒无限水平平均奖励MDP，提出无模型Q学习和actor-critic算法。证明了最优鲁棒Bellman算子在半范数下是严格压缩的，实现了与目标精度依赖的收敛界。数值实验验证了算法性能。

<h3 id="387">[387] Adaptive Reinforcement Learning for Unobservable Random Delays</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.14411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14411) (replaced)
- **Authors**: John Wikman, Alexandre Proutiere, David Broman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Delayed Feedback, Actor-Critic
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 标准RL假设无延迟，但实际系统中存在随机且不可观测的延迟。提出交互层框架，使智能体自适应处理延迟，并开发基于模型的ACDA算法。在运动控制基准上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="388">[388] On the Necessity of Output Distribution Reweighting for Effective Class Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.20893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20893) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Ebrahimpour-Boroojeny, Yian Wang, Hari Sundaram
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Membership Inference Attack
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CrowdDynamicsLab/ICML_TREW)
- **Summary**: 揭示类遗忘评估中忽略类别几何结构导致信息泄露的问题。提出TREW方法，通过估计类间相似度调整目标模型分布以近似重训练模型。实验表明TREW在多个基准上匹配或超越现有方法。

<h3 id="389">[389] Can Argus Judge Them All? Comparing VLMs Across Domains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.01042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01042) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Harsh Joshi, Gautam Siddharth Kashyap, Rafiq Ali, Ebad Shabbir, Niharika Jain, Sarthak Jain, Jiechao Gao, Usman Naseem
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有VLM基准排名忽略模型在不同数据集上的稳定性差异，存在能力-可靠性差距。提出ARGUS-EVAL框架，从能力、跨数据集一致性、鲁棒保持性和效率四个维度评估VLM。实验揭示了不同模型在能力与可靠性排序上的显著差异。

<h3 id="390">[390] Interaction Techniques that Encourage Longer Prompts Can Improve Psychological Ownership when Writing with AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.03670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03670) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nikhita Joshi, Daniel Vogel
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Prompt Engineering, AI-Assisted Writing
- **Venue**: Graphics Interface 2026
- **Summary**: 研究发现写较长的提示能提升用户对AI生成故事的归属感。设计了两种交互技术来鼓励长提示，实验表明这些技术增加了提示长度和归属感。添加AI建议进一步增加长度但未改善归属感。

<h3 id="391">[391] SWE-MERA: A Dynamic Benchmark for Agenticly Evaluating Large Language Models on Software Engineering Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.11059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11059) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pavel Adamenko, Mikhail Ivanov, Aidar Valeev, Rodion Levichev, Pavel Zadorozhny, Ivan Lopatin, Dmitry Babaev, Alena Fenogenova, Valentin Malykh
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有SWE-bench基准存在数据污染和测试不充分问题。提出SWE-MERA动态基准，通过自动化收集GitHub问题并严格验证质量，降低了污染风险。使用Aider代理评估多个最新LLM，展示了强大的区分能力。

<h3 id="392">[392] CRINN: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.02091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02091) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoya Li, Albert Wang, Guoyin Wang, Chris Shum, Jiwei Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search, Reinforcement Learning, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/deepreinforce-ai/CRINN)
- **Summary**: 近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在RAG中至关重要。提出CRINN，将ANNS优化视为强化学习问题，以执行速度为奖励，自动生成更快的ANNS实现。在多个基准上达到最优性能，验证了LLM+RL自动化算法优化的有效性。

<h3 id="393">[393] Beyond Naïve Prompting: Strategies for Improved Context-aided Forecasting with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.09904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09904) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Arjun Ashok, Andrew Robert Williams, Vincent Zhihao Zheng, Irina Rish, Nicolas Chapados, Étienne Marcotte, Valentina Zantedeschi, Alexandre Drouin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Context-aided Forecasting, Prompt Engineering, LLM Inference
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ashok-arjun/beyond-naive-prompting)
- **Summary**: 真实世界预测需要结合历史数据和文本上下文，但LLM存在执行差距、性能不足和计算成本高的问题。提出统一框架，包含诊断、准确性和效率四类策略。实验发现诊断策略揭示了执行差距，准确性策略提升25-50%，效率策略通过自适应路由降低推理成本。

<h3 id="394">[394] Nested-ReFT: Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning via Off-Policy Rollouts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.10123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10123) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maxime Heuillet, Yufei Cui, Boxing Chen, Audrey Durand, Prasanna Parthasarathi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Fine-Tuning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 标准ReFT框架在推理时计算成本高。提出Nested-ReFT，使用目标模型部分层作为行为模型生成离策略轨迹，减少推理成本。理论保证无偏梯度估计，实验表明在数学推理基准上提高了计算效率。

<h3 id="395">[395] PiCSAR: Probabilistic Confidence Selection And Ranking for Reasoning Chains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.21787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21787) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Zheng Zhao, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Sohee Yang, Wai-Chung Kwan, Xuanli He, Wenda Li, Pasquale Minervini, Eleonora Giunchiglia, Shay B. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Confidence Calibration
- **Summary**: Best-of-n采样选择最高奖励的推理链，但需要评分函数。提出PiCSAR，使用联合对数似然作为分数，无需训练。在数学推理基准上显著提升，分析表明正确推理链具有更高的推理和答案置信度。

<h3 id="396">[396] TENET: One Step Toward Test-Driven Development for Repository-Level Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.24148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24148) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiran Hu, Nan Jiang, Shanchao Liang, Yi Wu, Lin Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Software Testing
- **Venue**: ISSRE 2026
- **Summary**: 仓库级代码生成中测试驱动开发面临规格增强、检索增强和测试驱动细化挑战。提出TENET框架，包含测试集选择、智能体工具集和反思式细化。在RepoCod和RepoEval上显著提升Pass@1。

<h3 id="397">[397] Graph Optimization Foundation Model: Tokenizing Graph via A Language-Model Paradigm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.24256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24256) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunhao Liang, Pujun Zhang, Yuan Qu, Jingyuan Yang, Shaochong Lin, Zuo-jun Max Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Foundation Model, Combinatorial Optimization, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 将预训练-迁移范式扩展到图上的运筹优化问题面临组合约束挑战。提出图基础模型GFM，通过在随机游走路径上引入类似LLM的自监督预训练，内化图拓扑和组合规则。在多种距离优化问题上取得与专业求解器相当的竞争力，推理更快。

<h3 id="398">[398] Unveiling the Mechanisms of Multi-Hop Reasoning in Transformers via Identity Bridge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.24653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24653) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pengxiao Lin, Zheng-An Chen, Zhi-Qin John Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Hop Reasoning, OOD Generalization, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: COLM 2026
- **Summary**: LLM在多跳推理中分布内表现好但分布外失败。提出身份桥监督，强制对桥接token进行恒等映射，即使单层均匀注意力模型也能实现OOD推广。理论分析和实验表明身份桥诱导隐式正则化，建立直接的主语-答案关联。

<h3 id="399">[399] Toward Autonomous Soft Robotic Endovascular Navigation via Imitation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.09497](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09497) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Noah Barnes, Ji Woong Kim, Lingyun Di, Hannah Qu, Anuruddha Bhattacharjee, Miroslaw Janowski, Dheeraj Gandhi, Bailey Felix, Shaopeng Jiang, Olivia Young, Mark Fuge, Ryan D. Sochol, Jeremy D. Brown, Axel Krieger
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Imitation Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 血管内手术中软体机器人导丝导航困难。建立大规模2D投影环境，提出基于Transformer的模仿学习框架，包括目标条件、相对动作输出和自动造影剂注射。在未见几何结构上达到83%成功率，并在患者衍生几何上达到75%。

<h3 id="400">[400] People use fast and flat simulation to reason about new games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.11503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11503) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Katherine M. Collins, Cedegao E. Zhang, Lionel Wong, Mauricio Barba da Costa, Graham Todd, Adrian Weller, Samuel J. Cheyette, Thomas L. Griffiths, Joshua B. Tenenbaum
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Game AI, Reasoning
- **Summary**: 研究人类如何推理新游戏。通过大规模行为实验，发现人类在首次玩或评估游戏时表现出系统性和自适应理性。提出Intuitive Gamer计算认知模型，基于快速和扁平的目标导向概率模拟。

<h3 id="401">[401] Improving Topic Modeling of Social Media Short Texts with Rephrasing: A Case Study of COVID-19 Related Tweets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.18908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18908) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wangjiaxuan Xin, Shuhua Yin, Shi Chen, Yaorong Ge
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Social Media Analysis, Data Augmentation, Topic Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对社交媒体短文本主题建模效果不佳的问题，提出TM-Rephrase框架，利用大语言模型将推文重述为更标准化的语言，提升主题建模性能。实验表明，该方法在主题一致性、独特性和多样性上均有提升，尤其对LDA算法效果显著。

<h3 id="402">[402] Enabling Agents to Communicate Entirely in Latent Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.09149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09149) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhuoyun Du, Runze Wang, Huiyu Bai, Zouying Cao, Xiaoyong Zhu, Yu Cheng, Bo Zheng, Wei Chen, Haochao Ying
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Latent Space Communication
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XiaoDu-flying/Interlat)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体使用自然语言通信存在信息下采样损失的问题，提出Interlat范式，利用连续隐状态直接通信（潜在通信），并学习压缩过程。实验表明Interlat优于微调CoT和单智能体基线，并可将推理加速24倍。

<h3 id="403">[403] Enhancing Adversarial Transferability through Block Stretch and Shrink</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.17688](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17688) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Quan Liu, Feng Ye, Chenhao Lu, Shuming Zhen, Guanliang Huang, Lunzhe Chen, Xudong Ke
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Adversarial Attack, Adversarial Transferability
- **Summary**: 针对对抗性攻击迁移性不足的问题，提出FRO（前端响应导向）输入变换方法，通过局部缩放和投影操作生成结构化变换视图以优化可迁移对抗扰动。实验表明FRO在多种CNN和ViT模型上持续提升黑盒迁移性。

<h3 id="404">[404] SPQR: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Safety Alignment under Benign Model Adaptation</h3>

- **Tags**: Safety Alignment, Text-to-Image, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像扩散模型安全对齐在良性微调后（如LoRA）失效的问题，提出SPQR基准，统一评估安全、准确性、质量和鲁棒性。通过多语言、领域外等分析揭示安全对齐失败模式。

<h3 id="405">[405] On the Condition Number Dependency in Bilevel Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.22331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22331) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lesi Chen, Jingzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Bilevel Optimization, Convergence Analysis, Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对双层优化中条件数依赖的理论下界问题，建立了新的Ω(κ_y^(5/2) ε^(-2))下界，证明了双层优化与极小极大优化在条件数依赖上的首次可证明差距。

<h3 id="406">[406] CUDA-L2: Surpassing cuBLAS Performance for Matrix Multiplication through Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.02551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02551) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Songqiao Su, Xiaoya Li, Albert Wang, Guoyin Wang, Jiwei Li, Chris Shum
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/deepreinforce-ai/CUDA-L2)
- **Summary**: 针对矩阵乘法CUDA内核优化问题，提出CUDA-L2系统，结合LLM和强化学习自动优化HGEMM内核。在1000种配置上，CUDA-L2平均比cuBLAS快19.2%，比cuBLASLt自动调优快11.4%。

<h3 id="407">[407] The Theory of Strategic Evolution: Games with Endogenous Players and Strategic Replicators</h3>

- **Authors**: Kevin Vallier

<h3 id="408">[408] Graph-Based Bayesian Optimization for Quantum Circuit Architecture Search with Uncertainty Calibrated Surrogates</h3>

<h3 id="409">[409] Emotion Recognition in Signers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.15376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15376) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对手语识别者情感识别的挑战（语法与情感表情重叠及数据稀缺），提出eJSL数据集和跨语言方法，利用文本情感识别缓解数据稀缺，并证明手部运动可增强情感识别。

<h3 id="410">[410] MixFlow Training: Alleviating Exposure Bias with Slowed Interpolation Mixture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.19311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19311) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Generation, Exposure Bias
- **Summary**: 问题：扩散模型训练和测试之间的暴露偏差。方法：提出MixFlow训练方法，利用慢流现象中的减缓插值混合进行后训练。结果：在ImageNet 256x256上无需引导达到1.43 FID，带引导达到1.10 FID。

<h3 id="411">[411] SwinIFS: Landmark Guided Swin Transformer For Identity Preserving Face Super Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.01406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01406) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Habiba Kausar, Saeed Anwar, Omar Jamal Hammad, Ibrahim Radwan, Abdul Bais
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Super-Resolution, Face Recognition, Vision Transformer
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Habiba123-stack/SwinIFS)
- **Summary**: 针对人脸超分辨率中细节丢失和身份特征保留困难的问题，提出SwinIFS框架，结合地标热图先验和Swin Transformer，在CelebA上实现身份保留的超分辨率，尤其在高倍放大下效果显著。

<h3 id="412">[412] BiasLab: A Multilingual Dual-Framing Framework for LLM Bias Measurement, Applied to Workplace and HR Contexts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06861) (replaced)
- **Authors**: William Guey, Wei Zhang, Pei-Luen Patrick Rau, Pierrick Bougault, Vitor D. de Moura, Bertan Ucar, Jose O. Gomes
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Fairness, Bias Detection
- **Summary**: 针对LLM偏见评估方法碎片化的问题，提出BiasLab多语言双框架，通过镜像肯定和反向提示对测量方向性输出偏见。在10个模型、6个主题、12种语言上评估，发现所有模型表现出方向性偏好。

<h3 id="413">[413] Stable On-Policy Distillation through Adaptive Target Reformulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07155) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ijun Jang, Jewon Yeom, Juan Yeo, Hyunggyu Lim, Taesup Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Training, On-Policy Distillation
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 针对知识蒸馏中训练-推理分布不匹配及在线蒸馏不稳定问题，提出Veto方法，在logit空间构建几何桥梁，通过自适应梯度抑制稳定优化。实验表明Veto在推理和生成任务上优于监督微调和其他在线基线。

<h3 id="414">[414] Conditional Memory via Scalable Lookup: A New Axis of Sparsity for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07372](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07372) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xin Cheng, Rui Tian, Wangding Zeng, Damai Dai, Qinyu Chen, Bingxuan Wang, Zhenda Xie, Kezhao Huang, Xingkai Yu, Chengqi Deng, Shangyan Zhou, Chenggang Zhao, Zhewen Hao, Yukun Li, Han Zhang, Zhengyan Zhang, Yixu Wei, M.Y Xu, Huishuai Zhang, Dongyan Zhao, Wenfeng Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Architecture, Memory Architecture, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 针对Transformer缺乏原生知识查找原语的问题，提出条件记忆（Engram模块），通过O(1)查找的N-gram嵌入实现。在27B参数上，Engram在知识检索和推理任务上显著提升，并通过权衡MoE和静态记忆揭示了U形缩放定律。

<h3 id="415">[415] PUMA: Perception-driven Unified Foothold Prior for Mobility Augmented Quadruped Parkour</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liang Wang, Kanzhong Yao, Yang Liu, Weikai Qin, Jun Wu, Zhe Sun, Qiuguo Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Reinforcement Learning, Locomotion Control
- **Summary**: 针对四足机器人跑酷中实时适应和强化学习探索潜力受限的问题，提出PUMA端到端学习框架，集成视觉感知和足底先验，在仿真和真实环境中的复杂离散地形上展现出卓越的敏捷性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="416">[416] Referential Regimes: Transformation-Invariant Identity for Neutral Substrates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.16152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16152) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Denise M. Case
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Representation, Data Model, Philosophy of AI
- **Summary**: 针对数据系统在持续分歧下缺乏稳定参照的问题，提出中性基底的指称结构，通过变换不变性定义身份。推导出六种载体类型和至少九种身份体制，并给出条件性下界。

<h3 id="417">[417] SFO: Learning PDE Operators via Spectral Filtering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17090) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Noam Koren, Rafael Moschopoulos, Kira Radinsky, Elad Hazan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: PDE Solving, Neural Operator
- **Summary**: 该论文针对神经算子难以高效捕获偏微分方程解中的长程非局部交互问题。提出基于谱滤波的算子（SFO），利用希尔伯特矩阵特征模态构建通用谱基（USB）参数化积分核。在反应扩散、流体动力学和3D电磁学等六个基准上，SFO以更少参数实现最高精度，误差相比强基线降低高达40%。

<h3 id="418">[418] Rethinking Zero-Shot Time Series Classification: From Task-specific Classifiers to In-Context Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00620) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Juntao Fang, Shifeng Xie, Shengbin Nie, Yuhui Ling, Yuming Liu, Zijian Li, Keli Zhang, Lujia Pan, Themis Palpanas, Ruichu Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Classification, In-Context Learning, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/fangjuntao/TIC-FM)
- **Summary**: 该论文指出零样本时序分类中冻结编码器加分类器的做法违背无训练前提且引入偏差。提出TIC-FM框架，将标注训练集作为上下文，通过单次前向传播预测所有测试样本标签，无需参数更新。在128个UCR数据集上表现强劲，尤其在极少标签场景下持续提升。

<h3 id="419">[419] Disentangling Intrinsic Importance from Emergent Structure in Multi-Expert Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04291](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04291) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sudipto Ghosh, Sujoy Nath, Sunny Manchanda, Tanmoy Chakraborty
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Interpretability, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/parmanu-lcs2/inform)
- **Summary**: 针对多专家大模型系统中的编排策略不透明问题，提出INFORM解释性分析方法，将编排视为可分析计算，解耦交互结构、执行顺序和功能归因。实验发现路由频率不等于功能重要性；频繁选中的专家常为交互枢纽但影响有限，而稀疏路由的专家可能结构关键。消融实验证实INFORM能揭示超越准确率的依赖关系。

<h3 id="420">[420] Understanding Persuasive Interactions between Generative Social Agents and Humans: The Knowledge-based Persuasion Model (KPM)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11483) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Stephan Vonschallen, Friederike Eyssel, Theresa Schmiedel
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Persuasion, Social Simulation, Human-Agent Interaction
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨生成式社交智能体（GSA）与人类互动缺乏理论框架的问题。提出基于知识的说服模型（KPM），认为GSA的自我、用户和情境知识驱动其说服行为，进而影响用户态度。该模型为研究负责任GSA（如医疗、教育领域）提供结构化方法。

<h3 id="421">[421] SynthSAEBench: Evaluating Sparse Autoencoders on Scalable Realistic Synthetic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14687) (replaced)
- **Authors**: David Chanin, Adrià Garriga-Alonso
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Benchmark, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 该论文指出当前稀疏自编码器（SAE）基准噪声大或规模小且不真实。提出SynthSAEBench基准，生成具有相关、层次、叠加等真实特征的大规模合成数据，并提供真实特征和激活。该基准重现了LLM SAE的已知现象，并发现匹配追踪SAE可能利用叠加噪声而非学习真实特征来改善重构。

<h3 id="422">[422] Debiasing Central Fixation Confounds Reveals a Peripheral "Sweet Spot" for Human-like Scanpaths in Hard-Attention Vision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14834) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pengcheng Pan, Yonekura Shogo, Yasuo Kuniyosh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Eye Tracking
- **Summary**: 该论文发现常见眼动扫描路径指标受数据集中心偏差混淆，简单中心注视基线即可获得高分。提出中心去偏复合指标GCS（注视一致性分数），结合运动相似性。通过分析硬注意力分类器，揭示在中等斑块大小且同时使用中央凹和周边视觉时存在周边“甜区”，视野过大则进入捷径区域。

<h3 id="423">[423] BRIDGE: Bridging Reasoning In Distillation Gap Elimination via Structure-Aware Masking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17686) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bowen Yu, Sheng Zhang, Binhao Wang, Yi Wen, Jingtong Gao, Bowen Liu, Zimo Zhao, Shanshan Ye, Wanyu Wang, Maolin Wang, Xiangyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: SDM 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/SDM2026_BRIDGE)
- **Summary**: 针对将链式思维（CoT）推理蒸馏到小模型时能力不匹配的问题，提出BRIDGE课程框架：先通过掩码重构建立结构理解，再用GRPO强化学习让模型自主平衡准确性与简洁性，最后通过教师改写学习复杂推理。在GSM8K上，Qwen2.5-3B准确率提升11.29%，令牌减少27.4%，并在SVAMP和MATH-500上泛化。

<h3 id="424">[424] Turbo Connection: Reasoning as Information Flow from Higher to Lower Layers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17993) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohan Tang, Sidi Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文认为Transformer的推理能力受限于潜在计算路径的固定最大步数。提出Turbo Connection架构，将token t的高层隐藏状态通过多条残差连接路由到token t+1的低层。微调后模型在GSM8K、Parity等基准上准确率提升0.9%至10%以上；密集反向连接至关重要，使Qwen-3-1.7B在Parity上达到100%准确率。

<h3 id="425">[425] A General Equilibrium Theory of Orchestrated AI Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21255](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21255) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jean-Philippe Garnier
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Mechanism Design
- **Summary**: 针对集中编排的LLM智能体系统缺乏理论框架，建立了阿罗-德布鲁一般均衡理论，扩展至无限维商品空间。将LLM智能体建模为企业，编排者为消费者。证明均衡存在性、帕累托最优性、可去中心化性以及收缩条件下的唯一性；编排动态构成瓦尔拉斯调整过程。

<h3 id="426">[426] MetaState: Persistent Working Memory Enhances Reasoning in Discrete Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.01331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01331) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kejing Xia, Mingzhe Li, Lixuan Wei, Zhenbang Du, Xiangchi Yuan, Dachuan Shi, Qirui Jin, Wenke Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Reasoning, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文指出离散扩散语言模型（dLLM）存在“信息孤岛”问题，中间连续表示在去噪步骤间被丢弃，不利于推理。提出MetaState轻量递归增强模块，为冻结dLLM骨干网络添加持久工作记忆（混合器、更新器、注入器）。仅增加约0.6%可训练参数，在数学推理和代码生成基准上平均提升4.5个百分点。

<h3 id="427">[427] RVN-Bench: A Benchmark for Reactive Visual Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03953) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaewon Lee, Jaeseok Heo, Gunmin Lee, Howoong Jun, Jeongwoo Oh, Songhwai Oh
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Visual Navigation, Benchmark, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 现有基准忽略碰撞或不适合室内，提出RVN-Bench，基于Habitat 2.0和HM3D场景的碰撞感知室内移动机器人视觉导航基准。支持在线和离线学习，提供碰撞事件负轨迹数据集。评估表明训练策略能泛化到未知场景，并在Jackal UGV上初步实现仿真到现实迁移。

<h3 id="428">[428] VehAnchor: Metadata-Free Metric Scale Recovery from Vehicle Cues in Aerial Imagery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04277) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yifei Chen, Chenqian Le, Jiayi Cheng, Xupeng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Aerial Imagery, Object Detection, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 针对GPS拒止环境中无人机丢失元数据无法恢复尺度的问题，提出VehAnchor几何感知工具，通过车辆定向边界框估计像素长度，再用核密度估计和预校准参考长度恢复地面采样距离（GSD）。在DOTA v1.5上GSD中位误差6.87%；结合SAM进行面积测量，中位误差19.7%，分类依赖性降低2.6倍，灾难性故障减少4倍。

<h3 id="429">[429] Context-Dependent Affordance Computation in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04419) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Murad Farzulla
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Affordance Learning, Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/studiofarzulla/semantic-vision)
- **Summary**: 该论文量化了视觉语言模型中情境依赖的功能计算现象。使用Qwen3-VL等模型，发现超过90%的词汇场景描述是情境依赖的（Jaccard相似度均值0.095）；语义层面58.5%依赖。Tucker分解揭示了稳定隐因子（如“厨艺流形”、“访问轴”）。词汇与语义测量的差距表明表面词汇变化大于底层含义变化。

<h3 id="430">[430] Local Message-Passing for Discrete Graph Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.08825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08825) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jay Revolinsky, Harry Shomer, Jiliang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Generation, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对当前离散图生成依赖图Transformer或高阶架构的设计假设，提出GenGNN模块化消息传递骨干网络，通过潜在精化保留节点-边耦合状态。在标准基准上有效性超过90%，推理速度比图Transformer快2-5倍，且对生成去噪过程中的过平滑具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="431">[431] MUGEN: Evaluating and Improving Multi-audio Understanding of Large Audio-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.09714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09714) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chih-Kai Yang, Yun-Shao Tsai, Yu-Kai Guo, Ping-Le Tsai, Yen-Ting Piao, Hung-Wei Chen, Ting-Lin Hsiao, Yun-Man Hsu, Ke-Han Lu, Hung-yi Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/danielqwer/MUGEN)
- **Summary**: 针对大型音频语言模型（LALM）的多音频理解能力未充分探索，提出MUGEN基准，覆盖语音、通用音频和音乐。实验发现多音频场景下性能薄弱，且随音频数量增加急剧下降。提出音频排列自一致性，通过多样化音频顺序使预测更稳健，准确率提升6.28%，结合链式思维再提升至6.74%。

<h3 id="432">[432] Towards Robust Speech Deepfake Detection via Human-Inspired Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10725) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Artem Dvirniak, Evgeny Kushnir, Dmitrii Tarasov, Artem Iudin, Oleg Kiriukhin, Mikhail Pautov, Dmitrii Korzh, Oleg Y. Rogov
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Audio-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对当前语音深度伪造检测（SDD）泛化差且缺乏可解释性，提出HIR-SDD框架，结合大型音频语言模型与基于人工标注数据集的链式思维推理。实验证明该方法有效且能提供合理预测理由。

<h3 id="433">[433] ECoLAD: Selecting Anomaly Detectors for Automotive Deployment via Compute-Reduction Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10926) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kadir-Kaan Özer, René Ebeling, Markus Enzweiler
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Forecasting, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ITSC 2026 Industry Track
- **Summary**: 针对汽车部署中异常检测器选择仅依赖准确率基准而忽略实时CPU并行限制的问题，提出ECoLAD评估协议，定义单调计算缩减阶梯和显式CPU线程上限。实验表明，准确率稳定性与部署可行性可能分离：深度检测器保持AUC-PR但吞吐量下降，而轻量级经典检测器维持高评分率且优于随机基线。

<h3 id="434">[434] Evolutionarily Stable Stackelberg Equilibrium</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18385](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18385) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sam Ganzfried
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA; econ.TH; q-bio.PE
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Game Theory, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对现有Stackelberg进化博弈未明确强制执行抗突变入侵稳定性的问题，提出进化稳定Stackelberg均衡（SESS）概念，并给出离散和连续博弈的算法。实证验证了该概念在生物场景（如癌症治疗）中的应用。

<h3 id="435">[435] Uncertainty-guided Compositional Alignment with Part-to-Whole Semantic Representativeness in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22042) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hayeon Kim, Ji Ha Jang, Junghun James Kim, Se Young Chun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Uncertainty Estimation, Hyperbolic Representation
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jeeit17/UNCHA.git)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型（VLM）的欧几里得嵌入难以捕捉层次结构和多对象组合场景的问题，提出UNCHA方法，利用双曲不确定性建模部分到整体的语义代表性，并融合到对比目标中。在零样本分类、检索和多标签分类基准上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="436">[436] Critical Damping as a Momentum Schedule: Multi-Seed Validation, a Hybrid Recipe, and an Exhaustive Negative Result on Surgical Layer Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.28921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28921) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ivan Pasichnyk
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Training Dynamics, Hyperparameter Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/anthroos/research)
- **Summary**: 针对SGD中动量超参数调优问题，从阻尼谐振子模型推导出临界阻尼动量调度，实现2.34倍加速至90%测试准确率；混合配方消除精度损失。同时报告35种层组合的穷举负结果，表明梯度归因是避免伤害信号而非选择修复目标。

<h3 id="437">[437] StanceMoE: Mixture-of-Experts Architecture for Stance Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.00878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00878) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abdullah Al Shafi, Md. Milon Islam, Sk. Imran Hossain, K. M. Azharul Hasan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Stance Detection, Mixture-of-Experts, Text Classification
- **Venue**: LREC 2026 workshop proceedings
- **Summary**: 针对基于Transformer的立场检测难以捕捉异质性语言信号的问题，提出StanceMoE混合专家架构，包含六个专家模块和上下文感知门控机制。在StanceNakba 2026数据集上达到94.26%宏F1，优于传统基线和BERT变体。

<h3 id="438">[438] How Annotation Trains Annotators: Competence Development in Social Influence Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02951) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maciej Markiewicz, Beata Bajcar, Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz, Aleksander Szczęsny, Tomasz Adamczyk, Grzegorz Chodak, Karolina Ostrowska, Aleksandra Sawczuk, Jolanta Babiak, Jagoda Szklarczyk, Przemysław Kazienko
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Annotation, Human-AI Interaction, Social Influence Recognition
- **Venue**: AIED 2026
- **Summary**: 针对标注任务主观性且标注者判断可能演变的问题，研究社会影响力识别过程中标注者能力的发展。通过25位专家和非专家标注1021段对话，结合定性定量分析和LLM训练，发现标注过程提升标注者能力，且专家群体效果更显著。

<h3 id="439">[439] From Paper to Program: Knowledge Externalization and Bottleneck Diagnosis in AI-Assisted Quantum Many-Body Programming</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC

<h3 id="440">[440] Pickalo: Leveraging 6D Pose Estimation for Low-Cost Industrial Bin Picking</h3>

<h3 id="441">[441] MG$^2$-RAG: Multi-Granularity Graph for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对现有RAG系统难以处理跨模态推理且图方法成本高的问题，提出MG²-RAG多粒度图框架，构建层次化多模态知识图谱并融合实体与视觉区域。在四个多模态任务上达到最先进性能，同时实现43.3倍加速和23.9倍成本降低。

<h3 id="442">[442] Tool-MCoT: Tool Augmented Multimodal Chain-of-Thought for Content Safety Moderation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.06205](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06205) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="443">[443] Private Seeds, Public LLMs: Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对使用LLM生成私人文本合成数据需平衡隐私与效用的问题，提出RPSG方法，利用私有种子和差分隐私机制进行候选选择。实验表明其在与现有方法对比中实现高保真度和强隐私保护。

<h3 id="444">[444] Filtered Reasoning Score: Evaluating Reasoning Quality on a Model's Most-Confident Traces</h3>

<h3 id="445">[445] SegWithU: Uncertainty as Perturbation Energy for Single-Forward-Pass Risk-Aware Medical Image Segmentation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ProjectNeura/SegWithU)
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像分割中不确定性估计需重复推理或失败排序弱的问题，提出SegWithU后处理框架，利用冻结骨干网络和轻量级不确定性头，通过扰动能量建模不确定性。在ACDC、BraTS2024和LiTS上实现单次前向传播最佳AUROC，分别为0.9838、0.9946和0.9925。

<h3 id="446">[446] Learning in Blocks: A Multi Agent Debate Assisted Personalized Adaptive Learning Framework for Language Learning</h3>

<h3 id="447">[447] PivotMerge: Bridging Heterogeneous Multimodal Pre-training via Post-Alignment Model Merging</h3>

<h3 id="448">[448] Graph Construction and Matching for Imperative Programs using Neural and Structural Methods</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26578](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26578) (replaced)

<h3 id="449">[449] Toward a Scientific Discovery Engine for Weather and Climate Data: A Visual Analytics Workbench for Embedding-Based Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00972) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对机器人模仿数据多模态导致的帧条件化VLA策略跨块冲突问题，提出IntentVLA，一种历史条件化VLA框架，将近期视觉观测编码为短期意图表示并用于条件化动作块生成。在AliasBench等基准上提升了滚动稳定性并超越强基线。

<h3 id="451">[451] Prompt Compression in Diffusion Large Language Models: Evaluating LLMLingua-2 on LLaDA</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="452">[452] Faster Completion, Less Learning: Generative AI Reduced Study Time on Math Problems and the Knowledge They Build</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.21629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21629) (replaced)

<h3 id="453">[453] A Multi-Model Metric-based Selection Framework for Abstractive Text summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05494) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Ensemble Learning

<h3 id="454">[454] Let It Be Simple: One-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05737) (replaced)

<h3 id="455">[455] The Cross-Architecture Substrate: A Domain-Transcendent, Calibration-Surviving Geometric Invariant of Modern Vision Encoders</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对不同视觉网络应有不同表示但实际收敛到相同几何对象的问题，使用PCA等方法发现跨架构基板（前16个主方向）在13个编码器和8个域中通用。应用包括免标签迁移性过滤器、域检测器等。

<h3 id="456">[456] Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models</h3>

<h3 id="457">[457] ISE: An Execution-Grounded Recipe for Multi-Turn OS-Agent Trajectories</h3>

<h3 id="458">[458] Pipette: An Embodied Simulation Platform, Benchmark, and Data-Efficient Augmentation Framework for Wet-Lab Robotics</h3>

<h3 id="459">[459] Gefen: Optimized Stochastic Optimizer</h3>

<h3 id="460">[460] RealityBridge: Bridging Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting Driving Simulations and Real-World Videos</h3>

<h3 id="461">[461] RankGraph-2: Lifecycle Co-Design for Billion-Node Graph Learning in Recommendation</h3>

<h3 id="462">[462] FAST: A Framework for Aligned Sampling and Training in Parallel Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习中并行采样受拖尾效应和重新初始化延迟的问题，提出FAST同步并行框架，采用动态并行采样对齐和缩放掩码填充优化。在自动驾驶仿真中实现至少1.78倍加速，同时保持统计无偏性。

<h3 id="463">[463] Small edits, large models: How Wikipedia advocacy shapes LLM values</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.24890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24890) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jasmine Brazilek, Maria Navas, Alexa Gnauck
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY

<h3 id="464">[464] RWGBench: Evaluating Scholarly Positioning in Related Work Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.24894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24894) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anzhe Xie, Weihang Su, Jiaxin Mao, Yiqun Liu, Min Zhang, Shaoping Ma, Qingyao Ai
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Benchmark, Scientific Writing

<h3 id="465">[465] What Does It Mean to Break a Distillation Defense?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.25059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25059) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lena Libon, Pura Peetathawatchai, Michael Aerni, Daniel Paleka, Florian Tramèr
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对黑盒LLM的蒸馏攻击缺乏统一威胁模型的问题，提出一个包含查询预算、数据预算和接口配置三维度的威胁模型框架，并证明防御有效性依赖于假定的威胁模型。

<h3 id="466">[466] Average-Power-Budgeted Underwater Vehicle Control via Constrained Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.25680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25680) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinuo Wang, Gavin Tao, Yuze Liu, John V. Ringwood
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.ET; eess.SP; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotics, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对水下机器人能量预算有限的问题，提出将节能控制建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，使用PPO-Lagrangian算法显式约束平均推进功率。在三个平台和四个任务中，功耗降低14-65%，且无需手动调整权重。

<h3 id="467">[467] Helpfulness Hurts: Domain-Dependent Degradation of Mid-Trained Compassion Values Under Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26102) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jasmine Brazilek, Juliana Seawell
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Instruction Tuning, RLHF
- **Summary**: 研究后训练阶段中，有用性微调和编码微调对LLM动物同情价值观的不同影响，发现在Llama 3.1 8B上，有用性训练显著降低同情分数（SFT: 35.7% vs 65.2%），且该效应跨语言迁移。

<h3 id="468">[468] Assert, don't describe: Linguistic features that shift LLM reasoning about animal welfare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26104) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jasmine Brazilek, Harper Dunn
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 探究微调数据中语言特征对LLM动物福利推理的影响，发现断言确定性、道德词汇等特征增强亲动物福利立场，而模糊语言和感官描述则削弱。建议在训练语料中明确表达立场而非客观描述。

<h3 id="469">[469] NaviCache: Test-Time Self-Calibration Caching for Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26795) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zheqi Lv, Zhibo Zhu, Jinke Wang, Qi Tian, Shengyu Zhang, Zhengyu Chen, Chengxi Zang, Zhou Zhao, Fei Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Diffusion Acceleration
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对视频扩散模型计算开销大的问题，提出NaviCache，将特征演化重新建模为惯性导航问题，通过双状态估计架构在测试时自适应跳过计算。在HunyuanVideo等模型上实现更准确的计算跳过和综合性能提升。

<h3 id="470">[470] Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chenguang Wang, Ming Li, Xinyue Zeng, Zhuochun Li, Hong Jiao, Tianyi Zhou, Dawei Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Interpretability, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 解决人力题目难度预测依赖昂贵校准的问题，提出Epi2Diff框架，将大推理模型的推理轨迹映射为认知片段序列，提取片段动态特征结合语义表示进行预测。在SAT分类上比有监督微调平均提升8.1%。

<h3 id="471">[471] CMSL: Constructive Multi-Sequence Learning for Recommendation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28533) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zikun Cui, Renzhi Wu, Junjie Yang, Li Sheng, Jijie Wei, Linfeng Liu, Tai Guo, Tao Jia, Xiaodong Wang, Hong Li, Li Yu, Sri Reddy, Hong Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Sequential Recommendation
- **Summary**: 针对推荐系统中用户历史作为单一序列导致上下文污染的问题，提出构造性多序列学习（CMSL），将用户历史在隐空间解耦为多个主题序列，使用线性注意力高效建模。已部署于Meta的排序和召回任务。

<h3 id="472">[472] Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ananto Nayan Bala, Faisal Muhammad Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多工具/智能体路由问题，提出set-valued预测框架，基于WildChat构建3000条提示的基准，定义集上指标和约束路由评估。发现监督路由器优于零样本LLM，且加权路由层提升效能。

<h3 id="473">[473] Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.32027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Marcel Torne, Anubha Mahajan, Abhijnya Bhat, Chelsea Finn
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Reinforcement Learning, Reward Learning
- **Summary**: 针对长时域操作任务中奖励设计瓶颈，提出自由形式偏好学习（FPL），让标注者定义自然语言偏好轴并提供成对偏好，学习语言条件奖励模型。在四个真实任务上比稀疏奖励方法提升38个百分点。

<h3 id="474">[474] FLYNN: Robust Neural Network for Robot Navigation using Fly Brain Topology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00025) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Benquan Wang, Jingdao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Visual Navigation, Neuromorphic Computing
- **Summary**: 针对深度模型在环境变化时脆弱的问题，基于果蝇连接组构建递归神经网络FLYNN，在视觉导航任务中性能与现代网络相当，但具有更强的分布外鲁棒性和感官缺失容错。

<h3 id="475">[475] Diffusion-GR2: Diffusion Generative Reasoning Re-ranker</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01170) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhuoxuan Zhang, Kangqi Ni, Yuhang Chen, Mingfu Liang, Xiaohan Wei, Yunchen Pu, Fei Tian, Chonglin Sun, Frank Shyu, Adam, Song, Sandeep Pandey, Luke Simon, Tianlong Chen, Xi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对自回归推理重排序器推理慢的问题，将其转换为块扩散重排序器，通过转换微调和在策略蒸馏弥合精度差距。在Amazon Beauty上，解码吞吐量提升2.4-3.5倍，精度接近AR基线。

<h3 id="476">[476] DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunhe Li, Hao Shi, Wenhao Liu, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Zhongxiang Dai, Shuang Qiu, Linqi Song
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对在线策略自蒸馏中特权信息泄漏和过拟合问题，提出DemoPSD，通过选择性采用教师指导的逆KL重心目标，在SciKnowEval上优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持高训练熵和跨域泛化。

<h3 id="477">[477] Do All Visual Tokens Matter Equally? Object-Evidence Preserving Token Merging for Vision-Language Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04605) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Suhyeong Park, Junha Jung, Jungwoo Park, Jaewoo Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Token Reduction
- **Summary**: 针对多向量视觉-语言检索中图像侧标记冗余问题，提出SaMer，基于对象感知的标记合并框架，在训练时使用对象标注作为合并先验，减少93%标记，存储压缩16倍，并在Flickr30K上提升R@1。

<h3 id="478">[478] x-Prediction Is All You Need:Training-Free Accelerated Generation via Endpoint Decodability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.06114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06114) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xin Peng, Ang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Diffusion Sampling
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型采样步数多的问题，提出截断跳跃采样（TJS），利用端点可解码性在早期停止ODE并返回解码的x0，无需重训练。在SDXL上减少20-70% NFEs且质量几乎不变。

<h3 id="479">[479] AnchorPrune: Relevance-Anchored Contextual Expansion for Visual Token Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.07033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07033) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kyuan Oh, Bumsoo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Reduction, Pruning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MULTI-cau/AnchorPrune)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型中视觉标记冗余问题，提出AnchorPrune，先构建保护性相关性锚点，再通过重要性加权新颖性扩展上下文，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上使用160/2880标记保留97.6%性能。

<h3 id="480">[480] LieBN: Batch Normalization over Lie Groups</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.08783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08783) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziheng Chen, Yue Song, Rui Wang, Xiao-Jun Wu, Nicu Sebe
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Geometric Deep Learning, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GitZH-Chen/LieBN.git)
- **Summary**: 针对现有黎曼归一化方法局限于特定流形的问题，提出LieBN框架，利用李群上的不变度量进行批归一化。在9种几何上实例化，实验验证有效性。

<h3 id="481">[481] EHR-MPC: Inference-Time Control for Sepsis Treatment with Generative Patient Digital Twins</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.08793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08793) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joshua Pickard, Wei Qi, Na Li, Ann Woolley, Lisa Cosimi, Roy Kishony, Deborah Hung
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY; math.OC
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Reinforcement Learning, Digital Twin
- **Summary**: 该论文针对脓毒症治疗中现有强化学习方法策略固定、缺乏推理时适应性的问题，提出EHR-MPC框架。该框架通过训练生成式电子健康记录模型作为患者数字孪生，将学习患者动态与优化治疗解耦，并利用模型预测控制在推理时进行规划。在包含8家医院的多中心ICU数据集上，EHR-MPC取得了与强化学习基线相当的离线策略性能和更优的仿真性能。

<h3 id="482">[482] TACTIC: Tactile and Vision Conditioned Contact-Centric Control for Whole-Arm Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09218) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rishabh Madan, Angchen Xie, Samantha Saak, Andres Blanco, Dohyeok Lee, Sarah Grace Brown, Yunting Yan, Mark Zolotas, Jose Barreiros, Tapomayukh Bhattacharjee
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Multi-Modal Fusion, Model Predictive Control
- **Summary**: 该论文针对全臂操纵中臂构型与接触力耦合、接触状态部分观测、分布偏移等问题，提出TACTIC（触觉与视觉条件接触中心控制）框架。它结合RGB-D、分布触觉传感和2D临近表示，通过接触雅可比矩阵将学习的潜在动力学模型与分析运动学耦合，并利用采样模型预测控制进行接触感知动作规划。在仿真中TACTIC优于现有方法，并在真实机器人上完成三项全臂操纵任务。

<h3 id="483">[483] A Sovereign, Open-Source Foundation Model for German and English</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09424](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09424) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Soofi-Team, Benedikt Droste, David Fitzek, Ruben Härle, Lukas Helff, Maximilian Idahl, Alex Jude, Abbas Goher Khan, Maurice Kraus, Timm Ruland, Richard Rutmann, Sebastian Sztwiertnia, Markus Frey, Daniil Gurgurov, Jan Pfister, Tom Röhr, Sebastian von Rohrscheidt, Jörg Bienert, Nicolas Flores-Herr, Simon Gottschalk, Andreas Hotho, Kristian Kersting, Joachim Köhler, Alexander Löser, Wolfgang Nejdl, Simon Ostermann, Jan Plogsties, Patrick Putzky, Mehdi Ali, Michael Fromm, Max Lübbering
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Multilingual Learning, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 该论文提出Soofi S 30B-A3B——一个面向德语和英语的主权开源混合Mamba Transformer MoE基础模型，每token仅激活30B参数中的3B。在约27万亿tokens上预训练，刻意提高德语权重，在英语和德语基准测试上与140-270亿参数的密集模型相当，并在17个开源基模型中取得最佳代码聚合分数。模型完全开源，包括权重、数据记录等。

<h3 id="484">[484] Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions: A Feature-Based Graph Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09576) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kiran Pala, Punam Silu, Luxin Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Idiom Comprehension, Cross-Lingual, Graph Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对跨语言习语意义的可解释表示问题，提出基于特征图的概念网络框架。通过从认知语言学理论导出的二元概念特征（如包含、隐藏、情感等）构建加权图，利用Jaccard相似度进行社区检测。结果表明习语按概念模式而非语言聚类，该表示捕捉了分布嵌入中缺失的语义信息，提升习语检测性能，且跨语言迁移实验显示概念邻近性可识别翻译等价物，优于基于嵌入的基线。

<h3 id="485">[485] 4DR360: State Reasoning for Joint 3D Detection and Occupancy Prediction in 4D Radar-Camera Full-Scene Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09629) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaokai Bai, Lianqing Zheng, Runwei Guan, Songkai Wang, Siyuan Cao, Hui-liang Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Object Detection, Occupancy Prediction
- **Summary**: 该论文针对4D雷达-相机融合全景感知中检测与占用预测任务交互不足的问题，提出4DR360框架，将语义占用建模为持久场景状态进行推理。通过状态引导的BEV增强和多普勒引导的时间融合实现粗到细的特征聚合，并扩展ManTruckScenes数据集生成占用标签。实验在统一雷达-相机多任务评估框架下验证了准确性、鲁棒性和效率。
